# Skills · S · part 106

855 entries, seo-page-diagnosis to seo-strategist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **seo-page-diagnosis**: Diagnoses why one page underperforms in organic search through the Pilot MCP server: index state and canonical in Search Console, its queries, clicks, impressions and positions over time, an on-page audit (status, tags, load time, technical issues), the keywords it ranks for, and the live results for its main keyword. Use when a page lost traffic, never ranked, or ranks for the wrong queries. Triggers: why does this page not rank, page lost traffic, SEO page audit. Déclencheurs : pourquoi cette page ne se positionne pas, page qui perd du trafic, diagnostic SEO d'une page. ([BRAIKERS/pilot-mcp](https://github.com/BRAIKERS/pilot-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/seo-page-diagnosis))
